**Q:** why doesn’t Schwab allow extended hours for SPX trading, be brief

**A:**

→ **SPX options** are cash-settled index options restricted on Schwab platforms to regular exchange hours (**9:30 AM to 4:15 PM ET**)

→ **Retail infrastructure limits** prevent Schwab from supporting Cboe's overnight Global Trading Hours (GTH) for cash-settled index products

→ **Use /ES futures options** on thinkorswim if you need 24-hour overnight index exposure

**Q:** where do eye boogies come from

**A:**

→ **Rheum** (the medical term) is a natural mixture of **mucus (mucin)**, **oils (meibum)**, **sloughed skin cells**, and trapped **dust particles**

→ During waking hours, regular **blinking** continuously flushes this debris away across your tear film and down the nasolacrimal ducts

→ At night, the total **lack of blinking** allows the discharge to pool and dry out into crusty deposits in the corners of your eyes

Sources:
[1] [allaboutvision.com](<https://vertexaisearch.cloud.google.com/grounding-api-redirect/AUZIYQGdWRmEVxAZq_-yKsk0KSXinjQ2Rh4sHDjaJiAssTZPPdX6xCclYgRKRKhMi3m2adPwARnz5lLhFxv3xbqkjSXaXWuVKElkOOHvQqVH9jt4Br1gmtSurA2hDQqiuuplkHmrmvv_kEqITgu9kQtlm7dEYs6bICJGi-8zV5ckoebuMOiAfQ==>)

**A:**

→ **Shareholder vote on Dec 1, 2026:** Investors will vote on a proposed **1-for-20 to 1-for-30 reverse stock split** designed to address the NYSE minimum share price notice received after trading below $1.00.

→ **Market capitalization breach:** ACH also received a separate NYSE notice under Section 802.01B for dropping below the **$50 million** market cap and equity threshold, requiring an approved 18-month remediation business plan submitted within 45 days.

→ **Underlying operational drag:** Recent Q3 reporting showed revenue contracting year-over-year and full-year adjusted EBITDA tracking **up to 30% below prior guidance** amid persistent contract and margin headwinds.

→ **Likely trajectory:** While a reverse split can technically satisfy the exchange's minimum price rule, micro-caps burning enterprise value with depressed fundamentals typically experience severe post-split dilution and continued downside pressure unless cash flows stabilize.
